  patterns:
    - I think [clause].
    - I don't know [wh-clause].
    - I remember [doing].
    - Remember to [do].
    - I forgot to [do].
    - I realized that [clause].
    - I noticed [someone] [doing].
    - I consider [something] [adjective].
    - I'm considering [doing].
    - I expect [someone] to [do].
    - I wonder if [clause].
    - I decided to [do].
    - I learned how to [do].
  chunk_models:
    - structure: think about [noun/doing]
      meaning: 思考、考虑
      examples:
        - think about changing jobs
    - structure: think of [noun]
      meaning: 想到；想起
      examples:
        - think of a better way
    - structure: What do you think of [noun]?
      meaning: 觉得……怎么样
      examples:
        - What do you think of the idea?
    - structure: I think [clause]
      meaning: 认为……
      examples:
        - I think we need more time
    - structure: think of [doing]
      meaning: 考虑做某事
      examples:
        - think of moving abroad
    - structure: think twice

  exercises:
    enabled: true
    pipeline:
      - key: think-about-changing-jobs
        kind: chunk
        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 说考虑换工作
        chunk: think about changing jobs
        chunkMeaning: 考虑换工作
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 我最近一直在考虑换工作。
            answer: I've been thinking about changing jobs lately.
            hint: I've been thinking；lately
          - zh: 你认真考虑过换工作吗？
            answer: Have you seriously thought about changing jobs?
            hint: Have you；seriously
          - zh: 我现在还不想考虑换工作。
            answer: I don't want to think about changing jobs right now.
            hint: don't want to；right now
      - key: think-of-a-better-way
        kind: chunk
        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 说想到更好的办法
        chunk: think of a better way
        chunkMeaning: 想到更好的办法
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 我们得想出一个更好的办法。
            answer: We need to think of a better way.
            hint: need to
          - zh: 你能想出更好的办法吗？
            answer: Can you think of a better way?
            hint: Can you
          - zh: 我当时什么更好的办法都想不出来。
            answer: I couldn't think of a better way at that time.
            hint: couldn't；at that time
      - key: think-of-the-idea
        kind: chunk
        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 问对方觉得这个想法怎么样
        chunk: What do you think of the idea?
        chunkMeaning: 你觉得这个想法怎么样？
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 你觉得这个想法怎么样？
            answer: What do you think of the idea?
            hint: What do you think of
          - zh: 你觉得这个计划怎么样？
            answer: What do you think of the plan?
            hint: the plan
          - zh: 说实话，你怎么看这个建议？
            answer: Honestly, what do you think of the suggestion?
            hint: Honestly；the suggestion
      - key: think-we-need-more-time
        kind: chunk
        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 说觉得我们需要更多时间
        chunk: I think we need more time
        chunkMeaning: 我觉得我们需要更多时间
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 我觉得我们需要更多时间。
            answer: I think we need more time.
            hint: I think
          - zh: 我不觉得我们需要更多时间。
            answer: I don't think we need more time.
            hint: I don't think
          - zh: 你觉得我们需要更多时间吗？
            answer: Do you think we need more time?
            hint: Do you think
      - key: think-of-moving-abroad
        kind: chunk
        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 说考虑搬到国外
        chunk: think of moving abroad
        chunkMeaning: 考虑搬到国外
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 你有没有想过搬到国外？
            answer: Have you ever thought of moving abroad?
            hint: Have you ever；thought of
          - zh: 我从来没想过搬到国外。
            answer: I've never thought of moving abroad.
            hint: never；thought of
          - zh: 她正在考虑搬到国外工作。
            answer: She's thinking of moving abroad for work.
            hint: thinking of；for work
      - key: think-twice
        kind: chunk
        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 说做决定前要三思
        chunk: think twice before deciding
        chunkMeaning: 决定前三思
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 做那个决定之前你应该三思。
            answer: You should think twice before making that decision.
            hint: should；before making that decision
          - zh: 签合同前我得再好好想想。
            answer: I need to think twice before signing the contract.
            hint: before signing the contract
          - zh: 如果你真的在意她，就别急着下决定。
            answer: If you really care about her, think twice before deciding.
            hint: If you really care；before deciding
      - key: think-it-over
        kind: chunk
        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 说仔细考虑一下
        chunk: think it over
        chunkMeaning: 仔细考虑
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 让我仔细想想再回复你。
            answer: Let me think it over and get back to you.
            hint: Let me；get back to you
          - zh: 你不用马上决定，先好好想想。
            answer: You don't have to decide now—think it over first.
            hint: don't have to；first
          - zh: 我已经仔细考虑过了，还是想试试。
            answer: I've thought it over, and I still want to try.
            hint: I've thought；still want to try
      - key: think-ahead
        kind: chunk
        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 说要提前考虑
        chunk: think ahead
        chunkMeaning: 提前考虑
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 我们需要提前考虑并规划明年。
            answer: We need to think ahead and plan for next year.
            hint: need to；plan for next year
          - zh: 如果你提前想好，就不会这么慌。
            answer: If you think ahead, you won't panic like this.
            hint: If；won't panic
          - zh: 好的领导会提前想好下一步。
            answer: Good leaders think ahead about the next step.
            hint: Good leaders；the next step
      - key: think-clearly
        kind: chunk
        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 说脑子清楚不了
        chunk: think clearly
        chunkMeaning: 清楚思考
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 我累的时候脑子不太清楚。
            answer: I can't think clearly when I'm tired.
            hint: can't；when I'm tired
          - zh: 先冷静一下，我们才能想清楚。
            answer: Calm down first so we can think clearly.
            hint: Calm down first；so
          - zh: 压力太大时，她很难想清楚。
            answer: When the pressure is too high, she finds it hard to think clearly.
            hint: When the pressure is too high；hard to
      - key: think-highly-of
        kind: chunk
        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 说很认可某人的工作
        chunk: think highly of her work
        chunkMeaning: 高度评价她的工作
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 他们很认可她的工作。
            answer: They think highly of her work.
            hint: think highly of
          - zh: 老板其实很看重你的意见。
            answer: The boss actually thinks highly of your opinions.
            hint: actually；your opinions
          - zh: 我一直很认可他处理问题的方式。
            answer: I've always thought highly of the way he handles problems.
            hint: I've always；handles problems
      - key: think-for-yourself
        kind: chunk
        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 说要独立思考
        chunk: think for yourself
        chunkMeaning: 独立思考
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 你需要学会独立思考。
            answer: You need to learn to think for yourself.
            hint: need to learn to
          - zh: 别只听别人说，自己想想。
            answer: Don't just listen to others—think for yourself.
            hint: Don't just；—
          - zh: 这件事得你自己想清楚。
            answer: You'll have to think for yourself on this one.
            hint: You'll have to；on this one
      - key: think-about-listen
        kind: chunk
        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 听懂对方说在考虑某事
        chunk: think about
        chunkMeaning: 思考；考虑
        direction: en_to_zh
        items:
          - en: I've been thinking about what you said yesterday.
            answer: 我一直在想你昨天说的话。
            hint: I've been thinking；yesterday
          - en: She's thinking about quitting her job.
            answer: 她在考虑辞职。
            hint: quitting her job
          - en: Give me a minute to think about it.
            answer: 给我一分钟想想。
            hint: Give me a minute
      - key: think-opinion-listen
        kind: chunk
        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 听懂对方表达看法
        chunk: I think
        chunkMeaning: 我觉得；我认为
        direction: en_to_zh
        items:
          - en: I think we should wait a little longer.
            answer: 我觉得我们应该再等一会儿。
            hint: should wait；a little longer
          - en: I don't think he's right about this.
            answer: 我觉得他在这件事上不对。
            hint: I don't think；about this
          - en: Do you think this will work?
            answer: 你觉得这能行吗？
            hint: Do you think；work
      - key: think-state
        type: vocab_sentence_building
        title: 说看法和正在考虑
        vocabWord: think
        vocabMeaning: 认为；思考
        direction: zh_to_en
        patterns:
          - chunk: I think
            items:
              - zh: 我觉得我们应该早点出发。
                answer: I think we should leave earlier.
                hint: should leave earlier
              - zh: 我不觉得这是个好主意。
                answer: I don't think this is a good idea.
                hint: I don't think
          - chunk: think about it
            items:
              - zh: 让我想想再告诉你。
                answer: Let me think about it and tell you later.
                hint: Let me；later
              - zh: 你先别急着回答，再想想。
                answer: Don't rush to answer—think about it first.
                hint: Don't rush；first
          - chunk: think of
            items:
              - zh: 我当时什么都想不出来。
                answer: I couldn't think of anything to say.
                hint: couldn't；anything to say
              - zh: 你能想到更好的例子吗？
                answer: Can you think of a better example?
                hint: a better example
      - key: think-about-other
        type: pattern_drill
        title: 说考虑别的事情
        pattern: think about [noun/doing]
        patternMeaning: 思考、考虑
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 我最近一直在想明年的计划。
            answer: I've been thinking about next year's plan lately.
            hint: I've been thinking；lately
          - zh: 你有没有考虑过换个城市生活？
            answer: Have you thought about living in another city?
            hint: Have you；living in another city
          - zh: 先别想工作了，休息一下。
            answer: Stop thinking about work for a while and take a break.
            hint: Stop thinking；take a break
      - key: think-of-other
        type: pattern_drill
        title: 说想到别的东西
        pattern: think of [noun]
        patternMeaning: 想到；想起
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 你能想到他的名字吗？
            answer: Can you think of his name?
            hint: Can you
          - zh: 一提到夏天，我就会想到海边。
            answer: When I think of summer, I think of the beach.
            hint: When I think of；the beach
          - zh: 我想不出还有谁能帮忙。
            answer: I can't think of anyone else who can help.
            hint: can't；anyone else
      - key: think-clause-other
        type: pattern_drill
        title: 说别的看法
        pattern: I think [clause]
        patternMeaning: 认为……
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 我觉得他明天会来。
            answer: I think he'll come tomorrow.
            hint: he'll come；tomorrow
          - zh: 我觉得我们还可以再试一次。
            answer: I think we can try one more time.
            hint: can try；one more time
          - zh: 我不觉得问题有那么严重。
            answer: I don't think the problem is that serious.
            hint: I don't think；that serious
      - key: think-of-doing-other
        type: pattern_drill
        title: 说考虑做别的事
        pattern: think of [doing]
        patternMeaning: 考虑做某事
        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 你有没有想过自己创业？

        title: 把相信会好转说完整

        sourceText: believe that things will improve

            highlight: ''
            hint: 先说我仍然相信会变好
          - level: 2
            label: 加条件开头
            en: I still believe that things will improve if we keep trying.
            zh: 我仍然相信只要我们继续努力，情况会变好。
            highlight: if we keep trying
            hint: 补上只要我们继续努力
          - level: 3
            label: 完整句
            en: I still believe that things will improve if we keep trying and talk openly.
            zh: 我仍然相信只要继续努力、坦诚沟通，情况会变好。
            highlight: and talk openly
            hint: 补上并坦诚沟通

          - level: 3

      - key: understand-point-listen
        kind: chunk
        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 听懂对方说懂了或没懂
        chunk: I understand
        chunkMeaning: 我理解；我明白
        direction: en_to_zh
        items:
          - en: I understand—you don't have to explain again.
            answer: 我明白了，你不用再说一遍。
            hint: don't have to explain again
          - en: I don't quite understand what you mean by that.
            answer: 我不太明白你那儿是什么意思。
            hint: don't quite；what you mean
          - en: Please make sure everyone understands the new rules.
            answer: 请确保每个人都理解新规定。
            hint: make sure everyone
      - key: understand-state
        type: vocab_sentence_building
        title: 说听懂和表达理解
        vocabWord: understand
        vocabMeaning: 理解；明白
        direction: zh_to_en
        patterns:
          - chunk: I understand
            items:
              - zh: 我理解你的担心。
                answer: I understand your concern.
                hint: your concern
              - zh: 不完全明白，能再说一次吗？
                answer: I don't fully understand—could you say that again?
                hint: don't fully；could you
          - chunk: understand the situation
            items:
              - zh: 我们都要先摸清局面。
                answer: We all need to understand the situation first.
                hint: need to；first
              - zh: 她很快搞清了现场发生了什么。
                answer: She quickly understood what was going on.
                hint: quickly；what was going on
          - chunk: hard to understand
            items:
              - zh: 他口音重，有时候不好懂。
                answer: His accent is strong, so he's sometimes hard to understand.
                hint: accent is strong；sometimes
              - zh: 这份合同术语太多，不容易看懂。
                answer: This contract is full of jargon and hard to understand.
                hint: full of jargon
      - key: understand-noun-other
        type: pattern_drill
        title: 说理解别的事物
        pattern: understand [noun]
        patternMeaning: 理解（某事物）
        direction: zh_to_en

          - zh: 我们都必须理解这次改动的意义。

          - zh: 他还没理解这件事有多严重。

            hint: doesn't；how serious
      - key: understand-that-other
        type: pattern_drill
        title: 说理解别的事实
        pattern: understand that [clause]

        direction: zh_to_en
        items:
          - zh: 我明白我们不能事事都完美。

          - zh: 她终于懂了他当时别无选择。

          - zh: 你要让客户明白，价格已经是最低了。

            hint: need the client to
      - key: understand-why-other
        type: pattern_drill
        title: 说理解别的原因

        patternMeaning: 理解为什么……
        direction: zh_to_en

          - zh: 我完全理解你为什么生气。

          - zh: 没人能理解他们为什么要那么做。

          - zh: 读过报告后，你就会明白市场为何下滑。

      - key: understand-someone-other

        patternMeaning: 更理解某人
        direction: zh_to_en

      - key: make-understood-other

        title: 说在别的场合把意思说清楚

        patternMeaning: 让别人听懂自己的意思

      - key: fully-how-other

        title: 说弄懂别的东西怎么运作

          - zh: 我还没完全搞懂这笔钱怎么算的。

      - key: come-to-understand-other

          - zh: 她通过失败慢慢懂得了耐心有多重要。

      - key: easy-understand-other

      - key: understand-problem-build
        type: sentence_decomposition
        title: 把理解问题说完整
        sourceKind: chunk
        sourceText: understand the problem
        fullSentence: Once we understand the problem, we can focus on the right solution.
        fullSentenceZh: 一旦我们理解了问题，就能专注于对的解决办法。
        levels:
          - level: 1
            label: 核心句
            en: We need to understand the problem.
            zh: 我们需要理解问题。
            highlight: ''
            hint: 先说我们需要理解问题
          - level: 2
            label: 加时间条件
            en: Once we understand the problem, we can focus.
            zh: 一旦我们理解了问题，就能专注。
            highlight: Once we understand the problem
            hint: 补上一旦我们理解了问题
          - level: 3
            label: 完整句
            en: Once we understand the problem, we can focus on the right solution.
            zh: 一旦我们理解了问题，就能专注于对的解决办法。
            highlight: on the right solution
            hint: 把专注具体成对的解决办法
      - key: understand-point-build
        type: sentence_decomposition
        title: 把听懂对方观点说完整
        sourceKind: chunk
        sourceText: understand your point
        fullSentence: I understand your point, but I still think we should wait another week.
        fullSentenceZh: 我理解你的观点，但我还是觉得我们应该再等一周。
        levels:
          - level: 1
            label: 核心句
            en: I understand your point.
            zh: 我理解你的观点。
            highlight: ''
            hint: 先说我理解你的观点
          - level: 2
            label: 加转折
            en: I understand your point, but I disagree.
            zh: 我理解你的观点，但我不同意。
            highlight: but I disagree
            hint: 补上但我不同意
          - level: 3
            label: 完整句
            en: I understand your point, but I still think we should wait another week.
            zh: 我理解你的观点，但我还是觉得我们应该再等一周。
            highlight: wait another week
            hint: 把不同意具体成再等一周

      - key: remember-it-clearly

        type: chunk_substitution
        title: 说记得很清楚

        chunkMeaning: 记得很清楚

      - key: remember-correctly

        title: 说记得没错

      - key: remember-doing-done

          - zh: 她突然记起自己说过那句话，脸红了。

        direction: en_to_zh

      - key: remember-state

          - level: 2

            highlight: When I checked the figures again

          - level: 3
            label: 完整句

          - level: 2

            zh: 把它们并排比对后，我注意到了差别。
            highlight: Once I compared them side by side

          - level: 3
            label: 完整句

            zh: 把它们并排比对后，我立刻注意到了质量上的差别。
            highlight: in quality

        title: 听懂对方在往坏处想

        chunkMeaning: 往最坏想
        direction: en_to_zh

          - en: Try not to assume the worst before we get the test results.

          - en: Assuming the worst won't help us make a clear plan.

      - key: assume-state

      - key: imagine-internet-build
        type: sentence_decomposition
        title: 把想象无网世界说完整
        sourceKind: chunk
        sourceText: imagine a world without the internet
        fullSentence: Try to imagine a world without the internet—and how we would share news and photos.
        fullSentenceZh: 试着想象没有互联网的世界，以及我们还会怎样分享消息和照片。
        levels:
          - level: 1
            label: 核心句
            en: Imagine a world without the internet.
            zh: 想象没有互联网的世界。
            highlight: ''
            hint: 先说想象无网的世界
          - level: 2
            label: 加延伸
            en: Imagine a world without the internet and no social apps.
            zh: 想象没有互联网、也没有社交软件的世界。
            highlight: and no social apps
            hint: 补上也没有社交软件
          - level: 3
            label: 完整句
            en: Try to imagine a world without the internet—and how we would share news and photos.
            zh: 试着想象没有互联网的世界，以及我们还会怎样分享消息和照片。
            highlight: how we would share news and photos
            hint: 追问还会怎样分享信息与照片
      - key: imagine-five-years-build
        type: sentence_decomposition
        title: 把想象五年后的自己说完整
        sourceKind: chunk
        sourceText: imagine yourself five years from now
        fullSentence: Imagine yourself five years from now working in a role you actually enjoy every morning.
        fullSentenceZh: 想象五年后的自己，做着一份你每天早上都真心愿意投入的工作。
        levels:
          - level: 1
            label: 核心句
            en: Imagine yourself five years from now.
            zh: 想象五年后的自己。
            highlight: ''
            hint: 先说想象五年后的自己
          - level: 2
            label: 加状态
            en: Imagine yourself five years from now in a better job.
            zh: 想象五年后的自己处在更好的工作中。
            highlight: in a better job
            hint: 补处在更好的工作里
          - level: 3
            label: 完整句
            en: Imagine yourself five years from now working in a role you actually enjoy every morning.
            zh: 想象五年后的自己，做着一份你每天早上都真心愿意投入的工作。
            highlight: you actually enjoy every morning
            hint: 把工作说具体并强调每天早上愿意投入

      - key: expect-delays-build

        title: 把预料延误说完整

          - level: 2
            label: 加对象

            highlight: Passengers should

          - level: 3
            label: 完整句

            zh: 暴风要来了，乘客该预料到会有些延误，并多查更新。

      - key: expect-friday-build
        type: sentence_decomposition
        title: 把指望周五前做完说完整

        levels:
          - level: 1
            label: 核心句

            zh: 我们预计周五前能完成。
            highlight: ''
            hint: 先说预计周五前完成
          - level: 2
            label: 加条件
            en: We expect to finish by Friday if nothing slips.
            zh: 要是不再出岔子，我们预计周五前能完成。
            highlight: if nothing slips
            hint: 加上要是不再出岔子
          - level: 3
            label: 完整句
            en: We expect to finish by Friday if the vendor ships the parts on Wednesday.
            zh: 只要供应商周三把零件发出，我们预计周五前能做完。
            highlight: ships the parts on Wednesday
            hint: 把条件说成供应商周三发货

      - key: wonder-state

      - key: wonder-awake-build
        type: sentence_decomposition
        title: 把担心他是否醒着说完整
        sourceKind: chunk
        sourceText: wonder if he's still awake
        fullSentence: It's past midnight, and I wonder if he's still awake because he said he'd call me.
        fullSentenceZh: 已过午夜了，他说会打电话给我，我在想他是不是还醒着。
        levels:
          - level: 1
            label: 核心句
            en: I wonder if he's still awake.
            zh: 我想知道他是不是还醒着。
            highlight: ''
            hint: 先说想知道他是否还醒着
          - level: 2
            label: 加时间背景
            en: It's past midnight, and I wonder if he's still awake.
            zh: 已过午夜了，我想知道他是不是还醒着。
            highlight: It's past midnight
            hint: 补上过午夜这一背景
          - level: 3
            label: 完整句
            en: It's past midnight, and I wonder if he's still awake because he said he'd call me.
            zh: 已过午夜了，他说会打电话给我，我在想他是不是还醒着。
            highlight: because he said he'd call me
            hint: 补上他说会打电话的原因
      - key: wondering-help-build
        type: sentence_decomposition
        title: 把礼貌求助说完整

        sourceText: I was wondering if you could help

        fullSentenceZh: 讲座开始前，不知你能不能帮我弄一下投影仪。
        levels:
          - level: 1
            label: 核心句
            en: I was wondering if you could help.
            zh: 不知你能不能帮个忙。
            highlight: ''
            hint: 先用客气语气开口
          - level: 2
            label: 说清帮什么
            en: I was wondering if you could help me set up the projector.
            zh: 不知你能不能帮我弄一下投影仪。
            highlight: set up the projector
            hint: 补上具体要帮的事
          - level: 3
            label: 完整句
            en: I was wondering if you could help me set up the projector before the talk starts.
            zh: 讲座开始前，不知你能不能帮我弄一下投影仪。
            highlight: before the talk starts
            hint: 补上时间——讲座开始前

          - level: 3

      - key: evaluate-results-build

          - level: 3

          - level: 3

            highlight: confidently；overseas

          - level: 2

            highlight: If；things will improve

          - level: 3
